- **scvelo**: - **Start with stochastic mode** for exploration; switch to dynamical for final analysis - **Need good coverage of unspliced reads**: Short reads (&lt; 100 bp) may miss intron coverage - **Minimum 2,000 cells**: RNA velocity is noisy with fewer cells - **Velocity should be coherent**: Arrows should follow known biology; randomness indicates issues - **k-NN bandwidth matters**: Too few neighbors → noi ([mkurman/zorai](https://github.com/mkurman/zorai/tree/HEAD/skills/scientific-skills/scvelo) · ★327)
- **scvelo**: scVelo is the leading Python package for RNA velocity analysis in single-cell RNA-seq data. It infers cell state transitions by modeling the kinetics of mRNA splicing — using the ratio of unspliced (pre-mRNA) to spliced (mature mRNA) abundances to determine whether a gene is being upregulated or downregulated in each cell. This allows reconstruction of developmental trajectories and identification ([ComeOnOliver/skillshub](https://github.com/ComeOnOliver/skillshub/tree/HEAD/skills/K-Dense-AI/claude-scientific-skills/scvelo) · ★67)

- **scvelo**: Performs RNA velocity analysis with scVelo from spliced and unspliced single-cell RNA counts. - **scvi-basic**: scVI — probabilistic deep generative model for scRNA-seq integration and batch correction. Use for unsupervised integration across batches or samples, building a latent space for downstream analysis, or as a preprocessing step before scANVI. Requires raw counts. For semi-supervised annotation use scvi-scanvi; for multiome use scvi-multivi. ([tony-zhelonkin/scio](https://github.com/tony-zhelonkin/scio/tree/HEAD/skills/scvi-basic) · ★1)
- **scvi-continuous-covariate-correction**: Use when single-cell RNA-seq data contain quantifiable continuous nuisance covariates (e.g., cell-cycle or sex scores) and a latent-space batch-correction baseline is needed to compare against gene-level filtering methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scvi-continuous-covariate-correction))
- **scvi-contrastivevi**: contrastiveVI from scvi-tools — a contrastive deep generative model that splits scRNA-seq variation into salient (target-specific) and background (shared) latent spaces. Use for Perturb-seq, drug-vs-control, or disease-vs-healthy comparisons where confounding variation masks the effect. Needs a clear target-vs-background split and raw counts. ([tony-zhelonkin/scio](https://github.com/tony-zhelonkin/scio/tree/HEAD/skills/scvi-contrastivevi) · ★1)
- **scvi-covariate-validation**: Validate scVI batch correction covariate choices by comparing latent space quality metrics ([smith6jt-cop/Skills_Registry](https://github.com/smith6jt-cop/Skills_Registry/tree/HEAD/plugins/scientific/scvi-covariate-validation/skills/scvi-covariate-validation) · ★4)
- **scvi-framework**: scvi-tools framework foundation and router — installation, a model-selection decision tree, and the shared setup_anndata / train / get_latent_representation contract. Use first when choosing among the scVI variants, or when a cross-cutting concern applies to any scvi-* child (raw counts, batch keys, scArches flags, hub loading, GPU). ([tony-zhelonkin/scio](https://github.com/tony-zhelonkin/scio/tree/HEAD/skills/scvi-framework) · ★1)
- **scvi-hub-models**: Browses and loads pretrained scvi-tools models from the Hugging Face scvi-hub, and picks which scvi variant fits a modality (RNA, CITE-seq, multiome, ATAC, perturbation). Use when loading a HubModel, fetching a reference atlas checkpoint, or minifying a model. It does no surgery — for mapping, hand off to scvi-scarches-reference-mapping. ([tony-zhelonkin/scio](https://github.com/tony-zhelonkin/scio/tree/HEAD/skills/scvi-hub-models) · ★1)
- **scvi-lda**: AmortizedLDA (scvi-tools) for topic modeling on raw scRNA-seq counts, treating cells as documents and genes as words to find discrete transcriptional programs and per-cell topic proportions. Use for unsupervised gene-module discovery and transitional states. Needs raw counts. For continuous signed loadings use scvi-linearscvi. ([tony-zhelonkin/scio](https://github.com/tony-zhelonkin/scio/tree/HEAD/skills/scvi-lda) · ★1)

- **scvi-linearscvi**: LinearSCVI (a scvi-tools variant with a linear decoder) on raw scRNA-seq counts, giving continuous signed gene-to-factor loadings via get_loadings() — interpretable factors showing which genes drive each latent dimension. Use as a count-aware replacement for PCA-on-logcounts. No scArches support. For discrete topics use scvi-lda. - **scvi-mrvi**: MrVI from scvi-tools — multi-resolution variational inference for multi-donor scRNA-seq, modeling sample identity as biological rather than nuisance variation. Use for cohort studies needing cell-type-specific sample-to-sample distances and covariate-linked DE at single-cell resolution. sample_key is distinct from batch_key. ([tony-zhelonkin/scio](https://github.com/tony-zhelonkin/scio/tree/HEAD/skills/scvi-mrvi) · ★1)
- **scvi-multivi**: MultiVI jointly integrates paired and unpaired single-cell RNA and ATAC via scvi-tools, producing a shared latent embedding with cross-modality imputation. Use when combining 10x Multiome with RNA-only or ATAC-only datasets, or running DE plus DA on joint embeddings. For ATAC-only use scvi-peakvi. ([tony-zhelonkin/scio](https://github.com/tony-zhelonkin/scio/tree/HEAD/skills/scvi-multivi) · ★1)
- **scvi-peakvi**: PeakVI is a deep generative model in scvi-tools for scATAC-seq, producing batch-corrected latent embeddings and region-level differential accessibility. Use for standard scATAC clustering, batch correction across experiments, and scaling past a million cells. For paired RNA+ATAC use scvi-multivi; for TF activity use chromvar-motif-accessibility. ([tony-zhelonkin/scio](https://github.com/tony-zhelonkin/scio/tree/HEAD/skills/scvi-peakvi) · ★1)
- **scvi-prior-sampling-implementation**: Use when generating synthetic scRNA-seq observations with a trained VAE (scVI) but you want generation independent of the original observations, i.e., sampling directly from the prior of the latent space rather than the posterior. - **scvi-scanvi**: scANVI — semi-supervised deep generative model for scRNA-seq with partial cell type labels. Use when you have seed labels (full or partial) and need label transfer with better bio-conservation than scVI alone. Always initialize from a trained scVI model. For unsupervised integration use scvi-basic. ([tony-zhelonkin/scio](https://github.com/tony-zhelonkin/scio/tree/HEAD/skills/scvi-scanvi) · ★1)
- **scvi-scarches-reference-mapping**: scArches architectural surgery to map a query dataset onto an existing scvi-tools reference (scVI, scANVI, totalVI, MultiVI, trVAE): gene padding, adapter layers, fine-tuning with a frozen encoder, weighted-KNN label transfer, and novel-state detection. Use when the reference exists and you want to project queries without retraining. ([tony-zhelonkin/scio](https://github.com/tony-zhelonkin/scio/tree/HEAD/skills/scvi-scarches-reference-mapping) · ★1)

- **sd-pixel-processor**: SD Pixel Processor 节点使用指南。Pixel Processor 是 SD 中最强大的像素级计算节点， 可执行任意数学运算。包含：API 创建流程、函数节点完整参考、常用着色器模式。 Use when AI needs to: (1) create custom per-pixel effects, (2) implement mathematical texture generation (voronoi, noise, gradients), (3) build procedural patterns that atom nodes cannot achieve, (4) perform pixel-level color/value transformations. Substance Designer only (run_python). ([IvanYangYangXi/artclaw_bridge](https://github.com/IvanYangYangXi/artclaw_bridge/tree/HEAD/skills/official/substance_designer/sd-pixel-processor) · ★39)

- **sd-plan-delegate**: Burn down Seeds (sd) issues by delegation — a plan's children, a list of seed ids, or everything in `sd ready` that needs no human. Opus subagents work in parallel lanes (persistent git worktrees), you merge each result, gate the base branch, do the tracker bookkeeping and queue the follow-ups. Use when the user says "work plan pl-xxxx", "delegate this plan", "burn down the sd issues", "work through the issues overnight", or hands you a plan id or seed ids and wants long-running autonomous execution. - **sd-plan-tests**: The test-planning craft skill (software-delivery SDLC step 5). Run it AFTER front-authorization has frozen an atom's ACs and BEFORE implementation, to derive a per-AC test plan — happy / edge / negative cases plus the change-attributable baseline case for each pre-change checkpoint — mapped to the acceptance-contract checkpoints, and emit a test-plan note keyed to the AC-IDs. ADVISORY craft: it produces a plan, it never gates, approves, or merges; the merge floor (the adopter's branch protection + CI checks — see the plugin's docs/merge-floor.md) is the merge authority. ([lukasrepublic/agentic-foundry](https://github.com/lukasrepublic/agentic-foundry/tree/HEAD/skills/sd-plan-tests))
- **sd-planning**: Generate a feature plan through iterative problem breakdown, codebase research, and clarifying questions before handing off to tlc-spec-driven and requirements-table generation. - **sd-prompt-guide**: Stable Diffusion (Illustrious XL 等 SDXL 系) + Tsubaki / Mio 等 DiT 系 兩大架構的 Prompt 實戰指南。包含 Danbooru tag 資料庫、可協助使用者查詢 有效的 tag 和中英對照。 ⚠️ 第一步：先判斷使用者用哪一架構 - SDXL 系（Illustrious、NoobAI、Pony 等）→ Danbooru tag 寫法（本 SKILL 主路線） - DiT 系（Tsubaki、Tsubaki.2、Serin 等）→ 自然英文敘述（跳到 references/basics/16b-dit-prompt-tips.md） 兩條路線寫法完全不同、不要混用。 通用功能： (1) 協助使用者撰寫 prompt（任何主題） (2) 查詢 Danbooru tag 是否存在、使用次數、別名（僅 SDXL） (3) 中英對照查詢 (4) 模型推薦 (5) 常見實用小技巧 特別教學章節（模型較不擅長的領域）： (6) 畫男性角色（模型預設偏向女性） (7) 畫純風景/物件（模型預設會加入人物） (8) 多角色構圖控制（避免特徵混在一起） 進階技術： (9) LoRA 基礎與疊加技巧 (10) 在 PixAI 上訓練自己的 LoRA (11) 加速 LoRA（LCM、DMD2、PCM、WAI-Rectified） (12… ([wuguofish/wuguofish.github.io](https://github.com/wuguofish/wuguofish.github.io/tree/HEAD/sd-prompt-guide-docs/files/sd-prompt-guide))

- **sd-raw-rescue**: Rescata fotos RAW (Sony ARW/SR2, Nikon NEF, Canon CR2, DNG) y JPG de tarjetas SD/CF dañadas, borradas, formateadas o con el directorio roto, siempre en SOLO LECTURA y sobre una imagen 1:1 de la tarjeta. Rearma lo que PhotoRec/TestDisk dejan roto (bandas magenta, RAW que se ve bien en miniatura y se rompe al abrirlo, nombres con basura en DCIM, archivos de 0 bytes) usando la FAT y los volcados de directorio que quedan en la tarjeta, saca fotos borradas por cabecera TIFF y repara filas faltantes de un ARW con el JPEG embebido. - **sd-recon-engine**: 【套装内部技能 · 全流程用户通常无需直接调用；直接说需求给总控 short-drama-director 即可，总控会自动路由到本技能】【漫剧老李 V7.5.0-SLIM 套装 · 子技能 · 〇·1 素材来源锁 · 对标采风】素材采风与对标情报引擎（轻量版）：用户说「我要对标某个爆款」时，把该剧的公开信息从全网（含外网）找回来，按固定格式交付一份《对标情报卡》。核心能力——短剧信源分级清单（平台官方榜 / 数据指数站 / 行业媒体 / 社媒讨论 / 外网指数）、采风四步（定目标→定信源→检索→落卡）、对标情报卡十三项字段模板（含可迁移点与不可迁移点）、六条证据纪律（不编造 / 时点必标 / 来源必标 / 一手与转述分开 / 事实与推断分开 / 独立信源数才是热度参考）。触发词：对标爆款、扒一下、采风、找资料、收集信息、热点、爆款分析、参考这部、题材调研、品类调研、市场调研。本技能为套装成员，完整全流程从总控 short-drama-director 进入；〇·1 素材来源锁由总控选定。 ([lixiaoxiao9888-create/manju-laoli-skill](https://github.com/lixiaoxiao9888-create/manju-laoli-skill/tree/HEAD/sd-recon-engine) · ★2.2k)

- **sd-regression**: Catch unintended visual changes in the SMART Disburse onboarding — from colleagues' commits (shared checkbox, scripts, styles) or from our own work. Lists which commits reach SD pages, then screenshots every SD page and dialog before/after at 375/768/1280 and shows only what changed. Use at the start of the day, before taking in origin/main, before pushing, or when asked "what did &lt;colleague&gt; change that affects SD". ([vasileschidu/test-sx](https://github.com/vasileschidu/test-sx/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/sd-regression) · ★1)
- **sd-replan-loop**: Sanctioned mid-execution re-plan protocol for specwright. Lets a workflow adapt when execution (or the batch review) reveals that `02-tasks.md` is wrong, **without** violating spec immutability or skipping a gate. Referenced at runtime by `/sd:feature` and `/sd:refactor` - the only two workflows that produce a `01-plan.md` + `02-tasks.md` pair to re-plan. ([developzoneio/specwright](https://github.com/developzoneio/specwright/tree/HEAD/skills/sd-replan-loop) · ★10)
- **sd-repos**: Quick reference for SD/SIC (Sustainability Dashboard/Sustainability Insight Center) repositories. Use when answering questions about (1) which repositories are SD/SIC repos, (2) which repos are ADS apps, (3) where repositories are located locally or remotely, (4) repository categorization, (5) SD terminology equivalence, (6) management scripts in SD directory, or (7) deployment/Kubernetes information for SD applications. ([ketronkowski/skills](https://github.com/ketronkowski/skills/tree/HEAD/sd-repos))
- **sd-research**: Use when the user asks for deep, multi-source research on a question, topic, or writing piece and wants a verified, source-graded written brief or research notes rather than a quick answer. ([platypeeps/sd-workflow](https://github.com/platypeeps/sd-workflow/tree/HEAD/skills/sd-research) · ★1)
- **sd-research-repo**: Use when writing, rendering, reviewing, or publishing a document in a research repo that follows the shared research-repo standard — the numbered layout, provenance blocks, Status sections, and the outward mirrors. ([platypeeps/sd-workflow](https://github.com/platypeeps/sd-workflow/tree/HEAD/skills/sd-research-repo) · ★1)
- **sd-retro-lessons**: Lesson-extraction discipline for specwright. Turns retro prose into privacy-safe, reusable one-line rules that transfer to a codebase the author has never seen. ([developzoneio/specwright](https://github.com/developzoneio/specwright/tree/HEAD/skills/sd-retro-lessons) · ★10)
- **sd-review**: Test due system design concepts through bounded cold recall and changed examples. ([denelbjohn18/system-design](https://github.com/denelbjohn18/system-design/tree/HEAD/skills/review) · ★2)
- **sd-review**: Review the exact current diff with local providers and dispose of the findings locally, never posting them. ([platypeeps/sd-workflow](https://github.com/platypeeps/sd-workflow/tree/HEAD/skills/sd-review) · ★1)
- **sd-review**: 当用户希望审查代码时使用。即使用户只说"review 一下""检查代码""看看有没有问题""代码写完了""能不能合了""帮我看看""检查一下"也应触发。对照规格和任务计划检查 spec 合规性、代码质量，运行测试并做必要的 UI 验证。 ([wjgogogo/spec-dev-skills](https://github.com/wjgogogo/spec-dev-skills/tree/HEAD/spec/skills/review) · ★1)
- **sd-review**: The two-lens advisory review procedure the generic agent runs at the software-delivery review step (/foundry:sd-review, software-delivery review step). A PROCEDURE — it DISPATCHES the MERGED pr-reviewer general code lens (agents/pr-reviewer.md) ALWAYS, plus the security-reviewer security lens (agents/security-reviewer.md) WHEN the change touches auth/IAM, secrets/credentials, or supply-chain/dependencies, then collates the categorized findings for the operator. It does NOT re-implement review (the agents own the review craft). Advisory: it surfaces findings to the operator; it does NOT gate,… ([lukasrepublic/agentic-foundry](https://github.com/lukasrepublic/agentic-foundry/tree/HEAD/skills/sd-review))
- **sd-review-spec**: Review and correct a feature's spec documents (spec.md, design.md, tasks.md, plan.md, checks.md, requirements.md, context-map.md, ADRs) by verifying every claim against the real code, classifying gaps by severity, and applying fixes. Use when user says 'review this spec', 'audit the spec against the code', 'find gaps in the spec', 'check spec inconsistencies', 'find implicit requirements', 'validate design.md and tasks.md', or 'validate plan.md and checks.md'. ([Ronnasayd/aipp](https://github.com/Ronnasayd/aipp/tree/HEAD/skills/sd-review-spec) · ★3)
- **sd-roll**: Here is the reformatted content as a SKILL.md file: ([clodoaldoperito-droid/ARQUITETURA_JARVIS](https://github.com/clodoaldoperito-droid/ARQUITETURA_JARVIS/tree/HEAD/agent_skills/sd-roll) · ★1)
- **sd-run-flow**: Execute a SimplerDevelopment Workflow Designer agent flow — fetch the saved graph from the portal and actually run it, dispatching each node to its Claude Code persona subagent in graph order, pausing at human-in-the-loop nodes, and reporting live progress to the portal's Executions tab as it goes. Use when the user says 'run flow', 'execute the workflow', 'run the agent flow for project X', '/sd-run-flow', 'run the QA pipeline flow', or names a flow they built in the Workflow Designer canvas. Does not edit flows: it runs them and reports progress. ([SimplerDevelopment/SimplerDevelopment](https://github.com/SimplerDevelopment/SimplerDevelopment/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/sd-run-flow) · ★18)
- **sd-runtime-api**: Use when calling any sd.X identifier in a deck animation file, looking up the public surface of @whosejam/sd, choosing between Node subclasses, or picking a layout class from the layout() factory. ([whoseJam/sd](https://github.com/whoseJam/sd/tree/HEAD/packages/sd-agents/dist/claude/skills/sd-runtime-api) · ★13)
- **sd-sales-strategy**: Sales strategy and target setting ([Construct-AI-primary/agent-companies-paperclip](https://github.com/Construct-AI-primary/agent-companies-paperclip/tree/HEAD/doc/companies.bak/construct-ai/00887_sales-director/sd-sales-strategy) · ★1 · 3 copies)
- **sd-sales-tax**: Compute South Dakota 2026 sales and use tax, local sales and municipal gross receipts tax. Trigger on South Dakota sales tax, services, remote sellers, marketplace providers, groceries, digital products, EPath, use tax and contractor or agricultural boundaries. Load us-sales-tax first. ([openaccountants/openaccountants](https://github.com/openaccountants/openaccountants/tree/HEAD/agent-skills/sd-sales-tax) · ★498)
- **sd-sales-tax**: Use this skill whenever asked about South Dakota sales tax, South Dakota use tax, South Dakota DOR filing, South Dakota Wayfair, South Dakota municipal gross receipts tax, or South Dakota sales tax compliance. Trigger on phrases like "South Dakota sales tax", "SD sales tax", "SDCL §10-45", "South Dakota DOR", "Wayfair", "South Dakota grocery tax", "South Dakota SST", "South Dakota no income tax", or any request involving South Dakota sales and use tax compliance. ALWAYS load us-sales-tax first for federal context. ([3ero/openaccountants](https://github.com/3ero/openaccountants/tree/HEAD/agent-skills/sd-sales-tax))

([YuanyuanMa03/academic-research-skills](https://github.com/YuanyuanMa03/academic-research-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/sd-search/skills/sd-search) · ★80)
- **sd-search**: Search for academic papers on ScienceDirect. Use when the user wants to find papers by keyword on ScienceDirect/Elsevier. - **sd-ship**: Take committed work from a verified branch to a merged pull request, committing only enumerated paths. ([platypeeps/sd-workflow](https://github.com/platypeeps/sd-workflow/tree/HEAD/skills/sd-ship) · ★1)
- **sd-ship**: Ship SMART Disburse sandbox changes safely — scope check, bump the ?v= cache-buster on every page that loads a changed script/style, visual regression check against HEAD, commit, push to andrian-sandbox only, then hand over local links and a summary for Vasile. Use when Andrian says "fă push" / "push" / "ship", or before handing sandbox work over. ([vasileschidu/test-sx](https://github.com/vasileschidu/test-sx/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/sd-ship) · ★1)

- **sd-skill-adopt**: Intake an external skill through safety pre-screen, lint, canonical transform and provenance before writing it. ([platypeeps/sd-workflow](https://github.com/platypeeps/sd-workflow/tree/HEAD/contrib/sd-skill-adopt) · ★1)
- **sd-spec**: Refresh docs/spec on the PR branch so the repository's knowledge artifacts match what shipped. ([platypeeps/sd-workflow](https://github.com/platypeeps/sd-workflow/tree/HEAD/contrib/sd-spec) · ★1)
- **sd-spec-templates**: Per-template authoring rules for specwright. Used by `sd-spec-architect` when `TASK = create`. Each template has a dedicated section below. Read only the section matching the spec type being authored. ([developzoneio/specwright](https://github.com/developzoneio/specwright/tree/HEAD/skills/sd-spec-templates) · ★10)
- **sd-start-work**: Turn an approved Work Brief into a ready-to-run workspace - suggest a branch, create a git worktree with wto, write .work/&lt;key&gt;/PROMPT.md via sd-generate-prompt, and open it in a new tmux window. Use after triage when the user decides to start working on an item. ([mahmoudsalmi/.dotfiles](https://github.com/mahmoudsalmi/.dotfiles/tree/HEAD/dot_pi/private_agent/skills/sd-start-work) · ★1)
- **sd-status**: Read-only derived status for the repository you are standing in — is anything wrong, what is pending, what is next — with branch-protection enforcement gaps named. ([platypeeps/sd-workflow](https://github.com/platypeeps/sd-workflow/tree/HEAD/skills/sd-status) · ★1)

- **sd-storyboard-engine**: 【套装内部技能 · 全流程用户通常无需直接调用；直接说需求给总控 short-drama-director 即可，总控会自动路由到本技能】【漫剧老李 V7.5.0-SLIM 套装 · 子技能】文戏/日常戏分镜专精引擎（短剧视感版）：短剧视感机位体系 + 镜头经济性 + 表演克制 + 段内衔接5维度与运镜5模式，将小说/剧本原文转化为极简文字分镜脚本；**并产出故事板图（★标准出图资产）：每段一张多面板图，按动作相位七态拆格（起势/过程/接触/顶点/结果/反应/出口），格数由动作复杂度决定而非机械九宫格，出口帧=下段入口帧，CLEAN_GRID 干净版供生图 + 可选技术标注版**。**★ 须感知 I 画风锁影像基底（真人/2D/3D/水墨）并按其适配脸部特写角度——3D/仿真人禁「特写×平视正面」组合、改带角度侧视（真人守 1/3 配额、2D 同场连用 ≤2）**。触发词：分镜、文字分镜、电影分镜、生成分镜、帮我分镜、导演分镜、镜头脚本、storyboard、镜头衔接、运镜衔接、故事板、故事版、分镜图、故事板图、面板链、四宫格故事板、四格故事板、六宫格故事板、九宫格故事板、2×2 故事板。本技能为套装成员，完整全流程从总控 short-drama-director 进入。 ([lixiaoxiao9888-create/manju-laoli-skill](https://github.com/lixiaoxiao9888-create/manju-laoli-skill/tree/HEAD/sd-storyboard-engine) · ★2.2k)

- **sda-adr-bootstrap**: Cria o índice inicial de ADRs a partir das decisões arquiteturais existentes. Use ao adotar ADRs em um projeto. ([LuisFelipeUnzueta/spec-driven-agent](https://github.com/LuisFelipeUnzueta/spec-driven-agent/tree/HEAD/framework/skills/sda-adr-bootstrap))
- **sda-adr-create**: Cria uma ADR para decisão transversal, cara de reverter e com trade-off real. Use após decisão explícita. ([LuisFelipeUnzueta/spec-driven-agent](https://github.com/LuisFelipeUnzueta/spec-driven-agent/tree/HEAD/framework/skills/sda-adr-create))
- **sda-adr-deprecate**: Depreca uma ADR sem substituta e atualiza o índice. Use quando a decisão deixou de se aplicar. ([LuisFelipeUnzueta/spec-driven-agent](https://github.com/LuisFelipeUnzueta/spec-driven-agent/tree/HEAD/framework/skills/sda-adr-deprecate))
- **sda-adr-list**: Lista ADRs por status e escopo usando o índice do projeto. Use para localizar decisões arquiteturais. ([LuisFelipeUnzueta/spec-driven-agent](https://github.com/LuisFelipeUnzueta/spec-driven-agent/tree/HEAD/framework/skills/sda-adr-list))
- **sda-adr-reindex**: Reconstrói e valida o índice de ADRs a partir dos arquivos existentes. Use após mudanças ou inconsistências. ([LuisFelipeUnzueta/spec-driven-agent](https://github.com/LuisFelipeUnzueta/spec-driven-agent/tree/HEAD/framework/skills/sda-adr-reindex))
- **sda-adr-review**: Revisa qualidade, validade e aderência de ADRs ao código atual. Use em auditorias arquiteturais. ([LuisFelipeUnzueta/spec-driven-agent](https://github.com/LuisFelipeUnzueta/spec-driven-agent/tree/HEAD/framework/skills/sda-adr-review))
- **sda-adr-show**: Exibe uma ADR e suas relações com código, specs e decisões sucessoras. Use para consultar uma decisão. ([LuisFelipeUnzueta/spec-driven-agent](https://github.com/LuisFelipeUnzueta/spec-driven-agent/tree/HEAD/framework/skills/sda-adr-show))
- **sda-adr-supersede**: Substitui uma ADR aceita por uma nova decisão e atualiza relações e índice. Use após mudança arquitetural explícita. ([LuisFelipeUnzueta/spec-driven-agent](https://github.com/LuisFelipeUnzueta/spec-driven-agent/tree/HEAD/framework/skills/sda-adr-supersede))
- **sda-backend-contract-handoff**: Gera handoff verificável de contratos backend para consumidores frontend. Use após estabilizar endpoints e schemas. ([LuisFelipeUnzueta/spec-driven-agent](https://github.com/LuisFelipeUnzueta/spec-driven-agent/tree/HEAD/framework/skills/sda-backend-contract-handoff))
- **sda-challenge-spec**: Estressa uma Tech Spec ou Scope contra código, domínio e ADRs antes da decomposição em tasks. ([LuisFelipeUnzueta/spec-driven-agent](https://github.com/LuisFelipeUnzueta/spec-driven-agent/tree/HEAD/framework/skills/sda-challenge-spec))
- **sda-checklist-generate**: Gera e avalia checklist de qualidade para um artefato de especificação (PRD, INTENT, Tech Spec ou SCOPE), validando se os requisitos estão prontos para a próxima fase. ([LuisFelipeUnzueta/spec-driven-agent](https://github.com/LuisFelipeUnzueta/spec-driven-agent/tree/HEAD/framework/skills/sda-checklist-generate))
- **sda-curate-project-rules**: Avalia candidatos a regras e promove apenas padrões recorrentes, verificáveis e úteis ao projeto. ([LuisFelipeUnzueta/spec-driven-agent](https://github.com/LuisFelipeUnzueta/spec-driven-agent/tree/HEAD/framework/skills/sda-curate-project-rules))
- **sda-debt-resolution**: Transforma débitos registrados em uma versão de limpeza com tasks pequenas e validação proporcional ao risco. ([LuisFelipeUnzueta/spec-driven-agent](https://github.com/LuisFelipeUnzueta/spec-driven-agent/tree/HEAD/framework/skills/sda-debt-resolution))
